关于开展中国国际大学生创新大赛（2026）南方科技大学校内赛的预通知 关于开展中国国际大学生创新大赛（2026） 南方科技大学校内赛的预通知 为贯彻落实党的二十大和二十届历次全会精神，深入贯彻落实习近平总书记给中国国际大学生创新大赛参赛学生代表的重要回信和给大赛“青年红色筑梦之旅”大学生重要回信精神，落实《教育强国建设规划纲要（2024―2035年）》工作部署，打造教育、科技、人才一体推进实践平台，推动创新教育贯穿教育活动全过程，加强拔尖创新人才自主培养，加快推进科技成果转化落地，培育新质生产力发展新动能，为以教育强国建设支撑引领中国式现代化作出更大贡献，学生工作部就业创业中心拟定于2026年4月至6月举办中国国际大学生创新大赛（2026）（原中国国际“互联网+”大学生创新创业大赛）南方科技大学校内赛。现将有关事项通知如下： 一、大赛简介 中国国际大学生创新大赛（以下称“创新大赛”）是由教育部等12个中央部委和地方省级人民政府共同主办的重大创新创业赛事。大赛旨在以赛促教，探索人才培养新途径；以赛促学，培养创新创业生力军；以赛促创，搭建产教融合新平台。创新大赛受到了全国高校的高度关注和重视，其成绩对于学校综合排名等均具有较为显著的影响。在中国高等教育学会发布的《2024全国普通高校大学生竞赛分析报告》中，创新大赛再次位列第一位。 二、参赛对象 具有我校学籍的全体全日制在读本、硕、博学生和毕业5年内（即2021年之后毕业）的本、硕、博毕业生。 三、参赛赛道及组别 以团队为单位报名参赛。允许跨校组建团队，每个团队的参赛成员不少于3人，不多于15人（含团队负责人），须为项目的实际核心成员。参赛团队所报参赛项目，须为本团队策划或经营的项目，不得借用他人项目参赛。 （一）高教主赛道（含国际参赛项目） 根据参赛申报人所处学习阶段，项目分为本科生组（申报人和团队成员必须是本科生）、研究生组（申报人必须是研究生）。根据项目发展阶段，本科生组和研究生组均内设创意组、创业组，并按照新工科、新医科、新农科、新文科、人工智能+、低空经济、生物技术、量子科技、新能源、新材料设置参赛项目类型。 1.创意组 （1）参赛项目具有较好的创意和较为成型的产品原型或服务模式，在大赛通知下发之日前尚未完成工商等各类登记注册（大赛通知以教育部通知为准）。 （2）参赛申报人须为项目负责人，项目负责人及成员须为我校全日制在校本科生（不含在职教育）、研究生。 （3）学校科技成果转化项目不能参加本组比赛（科技成果的完成人、所有人中参赛申报人排名第一的除外）。 2.创业组 （1）参赛项目须已完成工商等各类登记注册（在大赛通知下发之日前注册）。 （2）参赛申报人须为项目负责人且为参赛企业法定代表人，须为普通高等学校全日制在校本科生（不含在职教育）、研究生，或毕业5年以内的全日制学生（即2021年之后的毕业生）。企业法定代表人在大赛通知发布之日后进行变更的不予认可。 （3）项目的股权结构中，企业法定代表人的股权不得少于10%，参赛团队成员股权合计不得少于1/3。 （二）“青年红色筑梦之旅”赛道 参加“青年红色筑梦之旅”赛道的项目应符合大赛参赛项目要求，同时在推进农业农村、城乡社区经济社会发展等方面有创新性、实效性和可持续性。根据项目性质和特点，分为公益组、创意组、创业组。 1.公益组 （1）参赛项目不以营利为目标，积极弘扬公益精神，在公益服务领域具有较好的创意、产品或服务模式的创新实践。 （2）参赛申报主体为独立的公益项目或社会组织，注册或未注册成立公益机构（或社会组织）的项目均可参赛。 2.创意组 （1）参赛项目基于专业和学科背景或相关资源，解决农业农村和城乡社区发展面临的主要问题，助力乡村振兴和社区治理，推动经济价值和社会价值的共同发展。 （2）参赛项目在大赛通知下发之日前尚未完成工商等各类登记注册。 3.创业组 （1）参赛项目以商业手段解决农业农村和城乡社区发展面临的主要问题，助力乡村振兴和社区治理，实现经济价值和社会价值的共同发展，推动共同富裕。 （2）参赛项目在大赛通知下发之日前已完成工商等各类登记注册，项目负责人须为法定代表人。项目的股权结构中，企业法定代表人的股权不得少于10%，参赛成员股权合计不得少于1/3。 （三）产业赛道 本赛道以团队为单位报名参赛，每支参赛团队只能选择一题参加比赛，允许跨校组建、师生共同组建参赛团队，参赛项目中的教师须为高校教师（2026年7月15日前正式入职，具体以教育部通知为准），团队成员须为揭榜答题的实际核心成员。 1.企业命题组 （1）针对企业开放创新需求，面向产业代表性企业、行业龙头企业、专精特新企业、新型研发机构等征集命题。企业命题应聚焦国家战略性新兴产业方向，倡导新技术、新产品、新业态、新模式。围绕新工科、新医科、新农科、新文科对应的产业和行业领域，基于企业发展真实需求进行申报。 （2）参赛团队所提交的命题对策须符合所答企业命题要求，命题企业将对命题对策进行契合度审核评价。参赛团队须对提交的应答材料拥有自主知识产权，不得侵犯他人知识产权或物权。 2.成果转化组 （1）聚焦高校科研成果转化应用与市场化推广，围绕核心技术专利转化、实验室成果产业化、产业链协同创新等方向，深化产教融合，促进创新链与产业链深度融合，推动更多高校科技创新成果转化落地，形成现实生产力的项目。 （2）成果转化组鼓励师生共同组建团队参赛，如已注册成立公司，公司注册年限不得超过5年（2021年3月1日后注册），师生均可为公司法人代表。企业法人代表在大赛通知发布之日后进行变更的不予认可。股权结构中，师生股权合并计算不低于51%，且学生参赛成员合计股份不低于10%。 3.产业赛道参赛安排 （1）征集命题。请命题企业进入全国大学生创业服务网（网址：https://cy.ncss.cn）进行中国国际大学生创新大赛（2026）产业赛道命题申报（具体截止时间以教育部通知为准）。 （2）命题发布。入选命题将在全国大学生创业服务网和全球青年创新领袖共同体促进会（PILC）官网（网址：www.pilcchina.org）公开发布。 （3）参赛报名。国内参赛团队通过登录全国大学生创业服务网进行报名。国际参赛团队通过登录全球青年创新领袖共同体促进会（PILC）官网进行报名（具体报名时间以教育部通知为准）。请命题企业及参赛团队登录全国大学生创业服务网，查看校企对接的具体流程，积极开展对接，确保供需互通。 四、参赛要求 （一）参赛项目能够紧密结合经济社会各领域现实需求，充分体现高校在新工科、新医科、新农科、新文科建设等方面取得的成果培育新产品、新服务、新业态、新模式，促进制造业、农业、卫生、能源、环保、战略性新兴产业等产业转型升级，促进人工智能、数字技术与教育、医疗、交通、金融、消费生活、文化传播等深度融合。 （二）参赛项目应弘扬正能量，践行社会主义核心价值观，真实、健康、合法。不得含有任何违反《中华人民共和国宪法》及其他法律法规的内容。所涉及的发明创造、专利技术、资源等必须拥有清晰合法的知识产权或物权。参赛项目如有涉密内容，参赛前须进行脱敏处理。如有抄袭盗用他人成果、提供虚假材料等违反相关法律法规或违背大赛精神的行为，一经发现即刻丧失参赛资格、所获奖项等相关权利，并自负一切法律责任。 （三）参赛项目只能选择一个符合要求的赛道报名参赛，根据参赛团队负责人的学籍或学历确定参赛团队所代表的参赛学校，且代表的参赛学校具有唯一性，在校生以在读学籍报名（以教育部通知下发之日为准），毕业生以最高学历报名。参赛团队须在报名系统中将项目所涉及的材料按时如实填写提交。 （四）参赛人员（不含产业赛道参赛项目成员中的教师）年龄不超过35岁（1991年3月1日及以后出生）。 五、活动安排 （一）比赛报名（即日起至5月15日） 师生科技成果转化项目、在校生及毕业5年之内校友的创业项目、“攀登计划”省级校级项目、大学生创新创业训练计划项目等各级各类创新创业项目及学科竞赛获奖项目、专业内创新实践项目、课程设计（Project）和毕业设计项目等，满足参赛条件的项目都可积极报名参赛。2026年度“攀登计划”省级、校级项目，按照立项承诺书需完成报名。 （二）初赛函评（5月中旬） 学校将邀请相关专家，采用线上评审方式遴选出参加校内赛的决赛项目。 （三）项目培育（5月下旬至6月上旬） 学校将邀请相关专家，开展项目训练营，对校内赛决赛项目进行打磨。 （四）决赛路演（暂定6月中旬） 学校将邀请相关专家，采用线下路演方式进行评审。 六、奖项设置 校内赛设置一等奖、二等奖、三等奖、优秀奖等若干奖项，不同奖项奖励相应的报销额度，校内赛获三等奖及以上可以等同于达到校级“攀登计划”项目结项要求；优秀作品更有机会被推荐代表学校参加创新大赛省赛。 七、报名方式 待教育部下发本年大赛通知后，参赛团队可通过登录全国大学生创业服务网（网址：https://cy.ncss.cn）进行报名，具体以教育部通知内容为准。学生操作手册可在“资料下载”板块下载。同时，请加入创新大赛交流群： 八、联系方式 阳老师：0755-88011976，jobs@sustech.edu.cn 学生工作部就业创业中心 2026年4月13日 Pre-notice on Holding The Career and Entrepreneurship Center of Students Affairs plans to hold the SUSTech Internal Competition of the China International College Students' Innovation Competition (2026) (formerly the China International \"Internet +\" College Students' Innovation and Entrepreneurship Competition) from April to June 2026. Relevant matters are hereby notified as follows: I. Introduction to the Competition The China International College Students' Innovation Competition (hereinafter referred to as the \"Innovation Competition\") is a major innovation and entrepreneurship event co-sponsored by 12 central ministries and commissions including the Ministry of Education and provincial people's governments. The competition aims to promote teaching through competition and explore new approaches to talent training; promote learning through competition and cultivate a new force for innovation and entrepreneurship; promote innovation through competition and build a new platform for industry-education integration.The Innovation Competition has received high attention and emphasis from universities nationwide, and its results exert a significant impact on the university's comprehensive ranking. In the 2024 National College Students Competition Analysis Report issued by the China Association of Higher Education, the Innovation Competition ranked first once again. II. Participants All full-time undergraduate, master's, and doctoral students currently enrolled at our school, as well as graduates with a bachelor's, master's, or doctoral degree within five years (i.e., those who graduated after 2021). III. Competition Tracks and Groups Registration shall be made on a team basis. Cross-university teams are allowed. Each team shall have no fewer than 3 members and no more than 15 members (including the team leader), all of whom must be actual core members of the project. The project submitted by the participating team shall be planned or operated by the team itself, and no project belonging to others may be used for participation. (I) Main Track of Higher Education (including international projects) According to the study stage of the applicant, projects are divided into the Undergraduate Group (applicants and team members must be undergraduates) and the Postgraduate Group (applicants must be postgraduates). According to the project development stage, both the Undergraduate Group and the Postgraduate Group consist of the Creative Group and the Entrepreneurship Group. Project types are classified by emerging engineering, emerging medicine, emerging agriculture, emerging liberal arts, AI+, low-altitude economy, biotechnology, quantum technology, new energy and new materials. 1. Creative Group (1) Participating projects shall feature sound creativity and relatively mature product prototypes or service models, and shall not have completed industrial and commercial or other types of registration before the issuance of the competition notice (the competition notice shall be subject to the notice of the Ministry of Education). (2) The applicant shall be the project leader, and the project leader and members shall be full-time current undergraduate students (excluding in-service education) and postgraduate students of SUSTech. (3) University scientific and technological achievement transformation projects shall not participate in this group, except those where the applicant ranks first among the completers and owners of the scientific and technological achievements. 2. Entrepreneurship Group (1) Participating projects shall have completed industrial and commercial or other types of registration before the issuance of the competition notice. (2) The applicant shall be the project leader and the legal representative of the participating enterprise, and must be a full-time current undergraduate student (excluding in-service education) or postgraduate student of an ordinary institution of higher education, or a full-time student within 5 years of graduation (i.e., graduates after 2021). Changes to the legal representative of the enterprise made after the issuance of the competition notice shall not be recognized. (3) In the project equity structure, the equity held by the legal representative of the enterprise shall not be less than 10%, and the total equity held by participating team members shall not be less than one-third. (II) \"Youth Red Dream Building Journey\" Track Projects in this track shall comply with the competition requirements and be innovative, effective and sustainable in promoting the economic and social development of agriculture, rural areas and urban-rural communities. We are the Student Branch of Southern University of Science and Technology Red Cross. On campus, we not only serve as advocates for first aid knowledge, but also as companions dedicated to safeguarding the physical and mental well-being of every student. To further enhance our campus mental health service system and provide professional support for those around us, the Student Branch is now officially launching recruitment for the Psychological Rescue Team. Psychological First Aid refers to the timely, non‑intrusive humane support provided to affected individuals in the aftermath of a crisis event. It aims to alleviate distress, stabilize emotions, and promote psychological recovery. The World Health Organization (WHO) defines it as “humane, supportive and practical help to fellow human beings who recently suffered exposure to a serious stressor,” emphasizing intervention in the initial phase of a crisis through non‑intrusive care, listening to needs, and safeguarding basic rights. The Psychological Rescue Team is a specialized and compassionate unit under the SUSTech Red Cross. Guided by the principles of \"prevention first, timely intervention, privacy protection, and scientific standards,\" and under the professional guidance of the University's Psychological Growth Center, we aim to provide timely and effective psychological support services for all faculty and students. Here, you will receive systematic training in psychological first aid and crisis intervention, empowering you to become a warm-hearted \"guardian of the soul\" on campus. In practice, you will embody the Red Cross spirit of \"humanity, impartiality, and voluntary service,\" experiencing the joy of helping others and achieving personal growth. To ensure the smooth operation of our work, we seek team members who are empathetic, dedicated, emotionally stable, mature-minded, and rational. Candidates should possess a strong sense of teamwork, comply with organizational arrangements, and commit the necessary time for training and service. By joining us, you will participate in comprehensive psychological first aid and crisis intervention training led by experienced psychological counselors, gaining practical and professional psychological skills. You will also have ample opportunities for hands-on practice, engaging in real-world scenarios such as campus psychological crisis intervention and mental health awareness campaigns, thereby continuously enhancing your capabilities and experience. Moreover, you will stand shoulder to shoulder with a group of passionate and service-minded peers, growing together through mutual support and forging cherished bonds of camaraderie and warm memories. If you are interested in this opportunity, please complete the application form (Attachment 1) and submit it by 23:59 on April 23, 2026. Subsequent interview arrangements will be announced via email and the recruitment QQ group (please see Attachment 2 for the QR code). Please stay tuned. If you have any questions regarding the Psychological Rescue Team, feel free to join the recruitment group for consultation. We look forward to welcoming compassionate and dedicated individuals like you to join us in building a warmer and more caring campus! Student Branch of SUSTech Red Cross April 20, 2026"

      "body": "研途茶话沙龙——研究生互助支持小社群招募成员 Research Journey Tea Talk Salon – Peer Support Small Community Recruitment 亲爱的研究生同学： 在求学的道路上，每一位研究生都可能在经历着一些挑战：或许你在为实验结果的不如意而感到焦虑挫败；或许你在为怎样处理与导师的关系而感到烦恼无措；或许为情感的求而不得而暗自痛苦；亦或为生活的单调，缺少与人的深度联结，感到孤独寂寞。当科研压力、人际困惑、情感需求交织成网，你是否渴望一个空间，能让你自由自在地与同路人坦诚交流，共享智慧与温暖？ 基于此，我们想打造这样一个专属空间--“研途茶话沙龙——研究生互助支持小社群”，现在开始招募小社群成员啦，欢迎感兴趣的研究生同学报名参加。 关于研究生互助支持小社群 这是由学校心理中心王玮老师发起的互助支持小社群，以轻松茶话的形式，汇聚8-12位硕博生，围绕学业压力、人际关系（含导生关系）、情感困惑、情绪管理、生涯发展等话题展开讨论与理解，我们会根据实际情况，利用一些心理学技术（如，家庭雕塑、角色扮演、身体调节、卡牌等）辅助理解问题并解构问题。在这里，我们不仅倾听彼此的故事，发展朋友，还会共同探索解决方案，在互助中成长。 小社群活动安排 本期互助支持小社群将在下周五开始，开展5次，每周一次，每次90分钟，活动时间为每周五下午16:00-17:30，在招募结束后将固定社群成员。小社群名额有限，请有意向的同学尽早报名。报名的同学敬请留意邮件，我们会邮件通知成功入选小社群的同学。 活动地点：学生心理成长中心团辅室（学生宿舍11栋205室） 招募对象 我校在读在校硕博生同学 带领者介绍： 王玮 南科大学生心理成长中心专职老师 中国心理学会注册心理咨询师 中国心理卫生协会注册心理咨询师 国家二级心理咨询师 国家高级职业指导师 从事大学生心理健康与教育工作十余年 报名方式：请扫描下附二维码或点击链接填写报名表 链接：https://www.wjx.top/vm/tU55HSl.aspx# 特别说明 •本社群为成长支持型团体，不涉及治疗性干预，侧重互助支持与赋能。 •正在接受医院心理治疗的同学请在报名时说明情况。 •需遵守团体契约，共同维护开放尊重的社群氛围。 •活动所需茶点需自备。 学生心理成长中心 2026年4月22日 Dear fellow graduate students, On the path of academic pursuit, every graduate student may encounter various challenges: perhaps you feel anxious and frustrated over unsatisfactory experimental results; perhaps you are troubled by how to navigate the relationship with your advisor; perhaps you suffer silently from unrequited feelings; or perhaps you feel lonely due to the monotony of life and a lack of deep connections with others... When research pressure, interpersonal confusion, and emotional needs intertwine, do you long for a space where you can freely and honestly communicate with peers, sharing wisdom and warmth? With this in mind, we want to create such a dedicated space—the “Research Journey Tea Talk Salon – Peer Support Small Community”. Recruitment is now open. Welcome all interested graduate students to sign up. ➢ About the Peer Support Small Community This small community is initiated by Ms. Wang Wei from the university’s Psychological Center. In a relaxed tea-talk format, it brings together 8—12 master’s and doctoral students to discuss and reflect on topics such as academic stress, interpersonal relationships (including advisor-student relationships), emotional confusion, emotion management, and career development. Depending on the actual situation, we will use certain psychological techniques (e.g., family sculpting, role-playing, body regulation, card tools, etc.) to aid in understanding and deconstructing problems. Here, we not only listen to each other’s stories and make friends, but also explore solutions together, growing through mutual support. ➢ Activity Arrangements This round of the peer support small community will start next Friday, consist of 5 sessions, once per week, 90 minutes each. The activity time is every Friday from 16:00 to 17:30. The group members will be fixed after recruitment ends. Spaces are limited, so please register as early as possible if you are interested. Registered students will be notified via email about successful admission. Location: Student Psychological Growth Center Group Counseling Room (Student Dormitory Building 11, Room 205) ➢ Target Participants Currently enrolled master’s and doctoral students of our university Facilitator Introduction: Wang Wei Full-time Counselor at SUSTech Student Psychological Growth Center Certified Psychologist of the Chinese Psychological Society Certified Psychologist of the Chinese Association for Mental Health National Level 2 Psychological Counselor (China) National Senior Career Advisor; Over 10 years of experience in university student mental health and education ➢ Registration Method Please scan the QR code below or click the link to fill out the registration form. Link: https://www.wjx.top/vm/tU55HSl.aspx# ➢ Special Notes This community is a growth-support group, not involving therapeutic intervention, focusing on mutual support and empowerment. Students currently receiving hospital-based psychological treatment are asked to indicate their situation when registering. Group agreements must be followed to jointly maintain an open and respectful community atmosphere. Tea and snacks for the activities are to be brought by participants themselves. Wishing you all the best. Counseling & Psychological Services 学生心理成长中心 南方科技大学/学生心理成长中心/公共邮箱 广东省深圳市南山区学苑大道1088号 二期学生宿舍11栋203-207"

7、活动组织方有权根据天气、人员情况对活动作出适当调整； 8、活动所有事宜解释权归赛事委员会所有。 十二、参赛提醒 1、提前学习如何避免和处理运动损伤，如水泡、抽筋、崴脚等； 2、体能不佳时及时休息，保持精神饱满。 3、带好比赛所需装备 十三、责任声明 本活动是具有一定负荷、强度、耐力活动，具有一定风险，对参加者身体状况有较高的要求，参加者应身体健康，有锻炼或训练的基础。有以下疾病患者不宜参加活动: 1、先天性心脏病和风湿性心脏病患者； 2、高血压和脑血管疾病患者； 3、心肌炎和其他心脏病患者； 4、冠状动脉病患者和严重心律不齐者； 5、血糖过高或过低的糖尿病患者； 6、两周内发热感冒者； 7、其它不适合运动的疾病患者。 十四、补充说明 (II) Participants shall ensure and prove the good health of all team members. VII. Registration Method 1. Participants are requested to register as a team using the registration form (Appendix 1). Members of the university badminton team are those currently in training; please mark them with an * during registration. The registration limit is 32 teams, accepted on a first-come, first-served basis. The deadline is May 16, 2026. 2. The registration form shall be sent as a file to the email of the contact person, Teacher Zeng, at zengym@sustech.edu.cn. The file name should be noted as team name + name of the event + registration form, for example: Team XXX + the 3rd Team Championship + Registration Form. 3. Upon successful verification of the registration, the team leader will be notified to scan the code to join the WeChat group and modify their nickname within the group. For example: Team Name + Name. VIII. Rules of the Competition 1. Events and Regulations of the Competition: This event is a badminton team championship, and the events for each team match include: men's singles, women's singles, men's doubles, women's doubles, and mixed doubles. Participants for this competition shall register as a team. A total of 32 teams will participate in the competition, based on the order of registration, and no additional teams will be accepted beyond 32 teams. Each team shall consist of 8 to 10 players, and no fewer than 8 players. It must include at least 4 male players and 4 female players. Among the 8 players participating in each team match, at most 1 male player and 1 female player from the university team are allowed to play, and they are not allowed to participate in multiple events. If a violation is reported, the team's results will be canceled. Members of the university team shall be marked with an *. For non-university team members, one female player in the team is permitted to participate in multiple events. 2. Rules of the Match The competition adopts a single-elimination format, without any seeded teams. The team matches as a whole adopt a best-of-five format (men's singles, women's singles, men's doubles, women's doubles, and mixed doubles), where each event also adopts a single 15-point game format, without extra points. The winning team of the current round of team matches will advance to the next round, and so forth. The 32 teams will compete against each other in pairs. The winning teams will advance to the next round until the champion team is determined. There will be a total of 5 rounds. In each round, the order of events for the team matches will rotate sequentially. The rotation order is as follows: Round of 32 to 16: Women's Doubles -> Men's Singles -> Men's Doubles -> Women's Singles -> Mixed Doubles Round of 16 to 8: Mixed Doubles -> Women's Singles -> Women's Doubles -> Men's Singles -> Men's Doubles Round of 8 to 4: Men's Doubles -> Men's Singles -> Mixed Doubles -> Women's Singles -> Women's Doubles Round of 4 to 2: Women's Doubles -> Women's Singles -> Men's Doubles -> Men's Singles -> Mixed Doubles Finals: Mixed Doubles -> Men's Singles -> Women's Doubles -> Women's Singles -> Men's Doubles (2) Planned Time of the Event No. Procedure Time Content 1 Check-in 8:00-8:20 Check-in 2 Official Competition 8:30-11:30 Official Competition 3 Award Ceremony 11:30—12:00 Award Ceremony (3) Planned Venue of the Event Runyang Badminton Hall at the Southern University of Science and Technology May 23, 2026, 08:30-11:30. Courts No. 1-4 and No. 7-10 will serve as the competition courts. IX. Methods of Reward For this event, teams ranked 5th to 8th will not play an additional playoff match and will tie for fifth place. The 1st and 2nd places will be the champion and runner-up, respectively, while the 3rd and 4th places will tie for third place. The event will provide rewards such as prizes and certificates of merit. The top three teams will be rewarded with prizes and certificates; the teams ranked 4th to 8th will be rewarded with certificates. X. Emergency Response Plan Emergency medical supplies, such as cooling sprays and first-aid kits, will be prepared on-site, and the badminton hall is equipped with an AED facility. If a player sustains a minor injury, it can be treated on-site before the player is sent to the Community Health Center. In the event of a severe injury, the student in charge from the badminton club on-site shall urgently contact the Community Health Center and dial 120 for emergency medical services. In the event of a conflict regarding the venue, the person in charge of the event on-site shall step forward to communicate and negotiate. If the negotiation fails, contact the staff of the venue reservation center or Teacher Zeng Yumin. In the event of a conflict among participants during the competition, the person in charge of the event on-site shall mediate. Depending on the severity of the situation, measures such as point deduction, cancellation of match results and qualifications, or referral to the respective college for handling shall be taken. XI. Notices for the Event If participants fail to participate in the match at the specified time (referring to the starting time established in the program), those who are absent or more than 10 minutes late for the match shall be deemed to have forfeited the current match. Compete with civility. Uncivil behaviors and verbally slandering others are prohibited. Participants must not be interfered with during the match. Participants must voluntarily join this event. They must be healthy individuals with full civil capacity and free from any diseases unsuitable for participating in this event, including but not limited to heart disease, hypertension, asthma, and mental illnesses. Organizing Unit sports center II. Registration Rules (1) Participations are voluntary and organized by individual academies. Each unit may submit one men's five-a-side team (12 players) and one women's three-a-side team (4–6 players). Each team shall have one team leader and one coach. (Note: The team leader shall be a designated faculty member from the academy.) (2) After registration confirmation, any team that replaces members without approval from the competition organizing committee will be disqualified from the competition. (3) To facilitate event management, athletes' jersey numbers shall not be altered or modified after each participating team submits their electronic registration forms. VI. Registration Method (1) Registration must be made at the college level. Team members may only register within their respective colleges; duplicate registrations or impersonation are prohibited. Any violation will result in immediate disqualification from the competition. Registration requires submission of both electronic and paper versions of the team application form. (II) Team Name. Participating teams may choose their own names in either Chinese or English, provided that the team name does not exceed 8 Chinese characters or 16 characters in total. The name must comply with Chinese laws, social ethics, and standards of spiritual civilization. VII. Competition Rules The competition adopts a group round-robin and crossover knockout format. The game lasts 40 minutes, divided into four quarters of 10 minutes each. The clock runs continuously during the first three quarters except for暂停 periods; it stops during the final quarter and the last two minutes of overtime. There is a 2-minute break between quarters and a 5-minute halftime break. Each quarter includes a 20-second quick stop, and暂停 from the second half can be carried over into overtime. Each overtime period lasts 5 minutes. VIII. Drawing of Lots and Joint Meeting The joint meeting is scheduled for Tuesday, April 28,2026, at 12:30 PM in the Sports Center Office (second floor of the Songhe Stadium stands). During the meeting, the teams will be randomly assigned to groups via on-site drawing, and the competition rules will be announced. Each participating team must send a representative or authorize an agent to attend. Teams unable to attend must notify Mr.Zhao Fei of the Organizing Committee by email before 11:00 AM on the day prior to the meeting; the drawing will be conducted by the Organizing Committee (email: zhao.f@sustech.edu.cn). The drawing results will be announced promptly. IX. Rewards The top four winners will receive certificates, and the champion will be awarded a trophy. X. Other Provisions (1) Competition Equipment. Participating units shall prepare their own uniforms and footwear. All participants must wear team uniforms (e.g., vests, aprons) as required by the Competition Organizing Committee during the event. One set each of dark-colored and light-colored uniforms is mandatory. (II) Competition Balls. The competition shall adhere to the latest \"Basketball Rules and Interpretations\" approved by the Chinese Basketball Association. During the match, standard size No.7 competition balls and 3x3 basketball competition balls shall be uniformly used. (3) Insurance. Strengthen safety assurance measures for the event to mitigate potential risk incidents. It is recommended that participating organizations arrange \"personal accident insurance\" for all team leaders, coaches, and athletes. (4) Participating units shall independently provide emergency supplies, first-aid kits, and medical equipment to address potential emergencies during their team competitions. (5) Participating organizations and athletes shall bear their own expenses during the competition. (6) The referees shall be appointed by the organizing unit. (7) Matters not covered herein shall be notified separately.       "body": "关于2026年深圳大运留学基金会申报材料提交的温馨提示 各位同学： 为确保2026年深圳大运留学基金会资助项目申报工作顺利推进，保障各位同学的申报权益，现将材料提交相关事宜温馨提示如下，请大家务必仔细阅读、严格遵照执行： 1. 线上材料提交：请各位申报同学对照项目申报通知要求，于4月27日12:00前，在报名系统完成所有申报材料（含视频）的上传提交，确保材料完整、无误。 2. 线下纸质材料提交：请将所有纸质版材料（视频除外），按时提交至南科大中心211办公室，提交时请核对材料顺序及完整性，避免遗漏。 特别提醒：申报视频请严格按照附件4《申请项目情况介绍视频制作要求》规范制作，若视频不符合要求，将予以驳回并要求重新修改，不仅影响申报进度，还可能给个人带来不必要的麻烦，请大家高度重视！ 本次申报后续还将涉及材料审核、学校评审、7天公示、评审报告及推荐表加盖校章并上传系统等一系列流程，时间紧、流程多。请各位同学务必严格遵守上述提交时间，逾期将无法补报，望大家合理安排时间，高效完成材料提交工作。 祝各位同学申报顺利、一切顺利！ 2026年4月24日 学生资助中心 Warm Reminder on Submission of Application Materials for 2026 Shenzhen Universiade International Scholarship Foundation Dear Students, To ensure the smooth progress of the 2026 Shenzhen Universiade International Scholarship Foundation funding project application and protect the application rights and interests of all students, we hereby issue a warm reminder on the relevant matters concerning material submission. Please read carefully and strictly follow the instructions: 1. Online Material Submission: All applicants are requested to complete the upload and submission of all application materials (including videos) in the registration system before 12:00 on April 27 in accordance with the requirements of the project application notice, ensuring that the materials are complete and correct. 2. Offline Paper Material Submission: Please submit all paper materials (excluding videos) to Room 211 of the Southern University of Science and Technology Center on time. When submitting, please check the order and completeness of the materials to avoid omissions. Special Reminder: The application video must be produced in strict accordance with the requirements specified in Annex 4 \"Video Production Requirements for Application Project Introduction\". If the video does not meet the requirements, it will be rejected and required to be revised. This will not only affect the application progress but also may bring unnecessary trouble to individuals. Please attach great importance to it! The follow-up of this application will also involve a series of processes, including material review, school evaluation, 7-day public announcement, stamping the school seal on the evaluation report and recommendation form, and uploading them to the system. The time is tight and the processes are numerous. All students must strictly abide by the above submission time; no late supplementary application will be accepted after the deadline. We hope you will arrange your time reasonably and efficiently complete the material submission. Wish all students a smooth application and all the best! Announcement of Shenzhen Universiade International Scholarship Foundation on Selection of Funded Students in 2026 关于遴选深圳大运留学基金会2026年 资助学生的通知 同学们： 深圳大运留学基金会（简称基金会）是深圳为弘扬大运精神，促进国际青年文化教育交流，特地筹集资金设立的一家专门资助留学生的公募基金会。 2011年，在深圳第26届世界大学生夏季运动会期间举行的世界大学校长论坛开幕式上，时任中共中央政治局委员、国务院副总理刘延东称赞深圳设立大运留学基金会的做法体现了深圳期望为各国大学合作与大学生交流贡献自己的力量，是世界大学生运动会的新亮点，也是世界大学交流的新举措；深圳市委、市政府主要领导宣布深圳大运留学基金会正式成立，为首批受助学生代表颁发资助证书。 根据深圳大运留学基金会的宗旨，为落实教育优先发展战略，培养国际化人才，促进世界对中华文化的了解，推动深圳与世界各地教育、文化、科技等方面的交流与合作，基金会启动2026年资助留学生遴选工作，与我校学生相关的公告内容如下： 一、资助项目、资助人数（指标）与资助标准 （一）个人申报项目（含学历教育、汉语言学习） 1.资助境外学生在中国境内高等院校接受全日制本科及以上学历教育项目，资助指标105名（我校可向基金会推荐不超过52名学生）； 2.资助境内高校学生前往国（境）外高等院校接受全日制硕士及以上学位教育项目，资助指标45名（我校可向基金会推荐不超过22名学生）。 （二）团队申报项目（高校短期国际交流） 3.资助在深办学高校全日制在校生组团（3人及以上）赴境外进行短期交流研究项目（1个月以上）； 4.资助境外高校全日制在校生到深办学高校进行短期交流研究项目（2周及以上）。 团队申报项目不单独设指标，资助金根据评审结果统筹安排，获准资助的项目1年内（2026年1月——2026年12月）有效。 （三）资助标准 1.国（境）外学生在中国境内高等院校接受全日制本科及以上学历教育的资助标准为：本科3万元人民币/人，硕士3.5万元人民币/人，博士4万元人民币/人。 2.境内高校学生出国（境）留学的资助标准为：硕士4.5万元人民币/人，博士5万元人民币/人。 3.高校短期交流项目不设定指标，根据评审结果和总经费预算统筹安排。 二、遴选标准及要求 （一）来中国境内留学受助学生 1.基本条件 （1）对华友好，品学兼优，形象良好，身心健康； （2）分布合理，兼顾男女比例。 2.就读要求 （1）学历教育申请人以本科二年级及以上学生为主，资助期内必须在中国境内连续就读1年及以上，未在境内就读者，不予受理。 3.优先范围 （1）来自“一带一路”国家、深圳国际友好城市留学生； （2）与中国文化、深圳科技产业相关专业背景的优秀来华留学生。 （二）出国（境）留学受助学生 1.基本条件 （1）热爱祖国，品学兼优，形象良好，身心健康； （2）分布合理，兼顾男女比例。 2.就读要求 （1）须持有境外高校录取通知书，并完成全日制注册，且资助期开始后（2026年9月起）在境外高校实际就读时长需连续满一年及以上。 （2）申请人须为境内高校毕业2年内的学生，毕业时间超过2年不予受理。 3.优先范围 （1）在海外留学的奥运会前8名的运动员； （2）“20+8”产业需求迫切相关学科专业的优秀学生； （3）西部地区、深圳地区及家庭经济困难的学生； （4）具有学成后回国服务强烈意愿的学生； （5）进入世界综合排名前100名大学学习的学生（参考软科世界大学学术排名）。 （三）申请短期交流项目的要求 高校短期交流研究项目须纳入申报高校年度对外交流计划。但不包括出国（境）交流研究时长≥1年，或是与国（境）外高等院校的交换生、交流生，联合办学期间出国（境）上课的学生。 （四）其他不具备申请资格的情况 1.曾获得本基金会同项目资助的学生。 2.2026年已享受中国境内同类奖（助）学金的学生，如国家留学基金管理委员会留学生资助金、广东省政府来粤留学生奖学金等。 3.延期毕业学生。 三、申报材料要求 （一）在中国境内接受全日制本科及以上学历教育留学生- -需提交以下材料 1.2026年遴选资助申报材料封面（含申请材料说明） 2.个人材料 必交材料：①《境外学生来中国境内留学资助申请表》、②有效身份证件（华裔须提供祖孙三代证明）、③录取通知书、④导师推荐信、⑤学业成绩单、⑥申请项目视频介绍 选交材料：⑦汉语水平证明、⑧近三年获奖经历、⑨发表的学术论文（文章）及专利证书 注：①④由学生工作部统一申请盖章，学生提交无盖章版的即可。 （二）出国（境）接受全日制硕士及以上学位教育的留学生- -需提交以下材料 1.2026年遴选资助申报材料封面（含申请材料说明） 2.个人材料 必交材料：①《国内学生出国（境）留学资助申请表》、②有效身份证件、③录取通知书、④导师推荐信、⑤学业成绩单、⑥申请项目视频介绍 选交材料：⑦外语水平证明、⑧近三年获奖经历、⑨发表的学术论文（文章）及专利证书、⑩家庭经济困难证明 注：①④由学生工作部统一申请盖章，学生提交无盖章版的即可。 （三）短期交流项目- -需提交以下材料 ①2026年遴选资助申报材料封面（含申请材料说明）、②申请报告书、③资助申请表、④有效身份证件、⑤邀请函（中英文对照翻译文本）、⑥申请资助项目的视频简介、⑦资助推荐表、⑧校内公示。 四、材料提交方式 符合资助条件的学生个人或短期交流项目负责人，于4月27日中午12:00前在报名系统上申请，并提交除视频外的所有纸质版申报材料。 1.线上申报：按照附件（1-2）学生端操作手册，登录报名系统（http://apply.suisf.org.cn/apaas/pc-login），按要求填写相关信息和上传申报材料。 2.纸质版材料提交： 提交至南科大中心211办公室 五、材料提交注意事项 （一）所有材料请勿使用订书钉装订，严格按照“三、申请材料要求”的顺序，统一使用长尾夹装订； （二）申报材料提交限制：申报材料应在规定时间内在网上提交，逾期不予受理；纸质版申报材料，将由学校存档备查，不会退还给学生本人； （三）导师推荐信：申报材料中的导师推荐信，须为申请人所在就读高校（或推荐学校）的在职教师针对基金会本年度资助项目申请作出的推荐； （四）所有签字均须手签。导师推荐信必须提交纸质版手写签字原件，请将推荐信打印出来交给导师，由导师手写签名后提交纸质版原稿； （五）需要有证明本人确定前往此学校深造的证明材料。如录取通知是临时offer，需将本科学位证明发送给深造学校后收到的确认录取通知邮件、或其他可以证明你确定不会更改升学去向的证明材料等一同提交。如果没有到申请的学校就读，即使之后获得了深圳大运留学基金会资助的资格，也无法发放资助金； （六）申请材料中如有英文材料，须附上中英文对照翻译版一并提交； （七）所有材料请务必按照“2026年遴选资助申报材料封面”中的申报材料说明提供，不符合要求将予以驳回。 更多详细信息请查看《深圳大运留学基金会2026年资助学生遴选公告》（附件1）。有关申报事宜，请咨询学生资助中心蒋老师，咨询电话88011971，咨询邮箱financialaid@sustech.edu.cn 附件：1.深圳大运留学基金会2026年资助学生遴选公告/Announcement of Shenzhen Universiade International Scholarship Foundation on Selection of Funded Students in 2026 2.遴选公告附件 党委学生工作部、学生工作部 2026年4月13日 Announcement of Shenzhen Universiade International Scholarship Foundation on Selection of Funded Students in 2026 Dear students: Shenzhen Universiade International Scholarship Foundation (“SUISF”) is a public-fundraising foundation established with specially raised funds to support eligible international students with a view to carrying forward the spirit of the Universiade and promoting cultural and educational exchanges among international youth. In 2011, at the opening ceremony of the World University Presidents Forum during the 26th Summer Universiade held in Shenzhen, Liu Yandong, then Member of the Political Bureau of the CPC Central Committee and Vice Premier of the State Council, spoke highly of Shenzhen’s effort to establish SUISF, as it reflected the expectation of Shenzhen to contribute to the cooperation of universities around the world and the exchanges of international students, calling it a new highlight of the Universiade and a fresh initiative for exchanges among universities around the world. On this occasion, the leaders of the Shenzhen Municipal CPC Committee and Shenzhen Municipal People’s Government announced the formal establishment of SUISF, and presented the funding certificates to the first group of representatives of funded students. In accordance with the purpose of SUISF to pursue the strategy of prioritizing the development of education, cultivate international talents, enhance the world’s understanding of the Chinese culture, and promote exchanges and cooperation between Shenzhen and the world in the fields of education, culture, and science and technology, SUISF has initiated the selection of international students to be funded in 2026. The relevant announcements for our university's students are as follows: I. Funding Programs, Quotas and Funding Criteria (I) Programs for Individual Application (Including Academic Education and Studies of Chinese Language and Literature) 1. To fund 105 overseas students pursuing full-time undergraduate education or above at colleges and universities in Chinese Mainland (Our university may recommend up to 52 students to the Foundation); 2. To fund 45 Chinese students from the Chinese Mainland to pursue a full-time education for a master’s degree or above at foreign colleges and universities (Our university may recommend up to 22 students to the Foundation). (II) Programs for Group Application (Including Short-Term International Exchanges for Students from Colleges and Universities) 3. To fund groups of full-time students (3 or more students per group) from colleges and universities in Shenzhen to go overseas for short-term exchange and research programs (each for more than 1 month); 4. ...(truncated)"

You are cordially invited to attend. We will provide a systematic overview of topics, including AI academic ethics, academic AI tools and AI research platforms, literature research in science and technology, preprints and open access resources, the reference management software Zotero, and journal evaluation tools and submission guidelines. Through case studies and practical demonstrations, this program aims to help you make better use of library resources, learn to use academic AI tools efficiently and responsibly, and enhance your research literacy. &nbsp; The 7-session training will run from 28 April to 20 May, taking place at the same time and place every Tuesday and Wednesday afternoon; the eighth session will be a student sharing and interaction session. Further details are provided below. We look forward to your participation! &nbsp; Topics and Dates: Session Topic Date Session 1 The Boundaries and Ethics of AI in Academic Research April 28 (Tuesday) Session 2 Efficient Utilization of Academic AI Tools April 29 (Wednesday) Session 3 Literature Review of Research Proposal for Subjects of Science and Technology May 6 (Wednesday) Session 4 Introduction to Preprints and Open Access Resources May 12 (Tuesday) Session 5 AI Research Platform: Bohr Science Navigator for Efficiency Improvement May 13 (Wednesday) Session 6 Reference Management Software Zotero 9 – A Quick Start May 19 (Tuesday) Session 7 Journal Evaluation Tools and Manuscript Submission Guidance in the AI Era May 20 (Wednesday) Session 8 (Student Sharing and Interaction) May 26 (Tuesday) Time:&nbsp;16:20–17:20 Venue: Lecture Hall 301, Yidan Library Speaker:&nbsp;Library Subject Librarians Language:&nbsp;Chinese Service, Innovation, User-Centeredness, Collaboration https://lib.sustech.edu.cn &nbsp;"

七、参赛办法 （一）实行网上推送问卷报名。各参赛人员需要扫码填写报名问卷，于 2026 年 5 月 9 日 11：59 前完成报名。 （二）赛事组委会将按照问卷提交时间先后顺序和问卷意向参加时间，选取前 64 位报名同学（初级段位 32 名，中级段位 32 名）参加本次通级达标段位赛。 （三）所有参赛运动员必须为身体健康者，如遇意外受伤、感觉不适者无法完成比赛，参赛人员可选择退出比赛。比赛中如遇其他情况，由组委会负责处置。 （四）具有参赛资格的个人，如因故未能按时完成报名，则视为弃权，不再递补。 （五）成功报名通级达标赛的个人，如因故未能准时参赛，应及时申请取消报名，否则将计入系列赛事诚信档案。如需取消通级达标赛报名，请于 2026 年 5 月 9 日 11：59 前联系邮箱：12412207@mail.sustech.edu.cn。 八、竞赛办法 （一）比赛执行《南方科技大学乒乓球技能等级（段位）考核实施办法（试行）》、《南方科技大学乒乓球技能等级（初级）评价标准》和《南方科技大学乒乓球技能等级（中级）评价标准》。 本次比赛按选手已通过的乒乓球技能等级（段位）分为初级组、中级组，各组别独立组织比赛，选手须报名参加与自身段位对应的组别。具体规则如下： 组别划分与参赛资格 分组原则：根据《南方科技大学乒乓球技能等级（段位）考核实施办法（试行）》，参赛选手按已通过的段位归入对应组别： 初级组：通过初级段位（含初级 1 段、初级 2 段、初级 3 段）的选手； 中级组：通过中级段位（含中级 1 段、中级 2 段、中级 3 段）的选手； 未通过任何段位考核的选手需先参加通级达标，达标后可报名对应组别。 报名要求：每位选手仅限参加一个组别，不得跨组参赛。 初级组混合单打规则 （1）赛制与晋级规则 赛制：采用三局两胜制，每局 11 分制（让分后先得 11 分者胜，需领先对手 2 分及以上）。 赛制流程：全程采用单败淘汰赛，所有选手不分男女，通过抽签确定对阵顺序，胜者晋级下一轮，败者淘汰，直至决出前 8 名并进行排名。 种子设置：不设种子选手，抽签结果公平随机。 （2）让分规则 性别让分：当男选手对阵女选手时，男选手每局让 2 分。 段位让分：同组别内，高段位选手对阵低段位选手时，每高 1 段让 2 分（如初级 3 段 vs 初级 1 段，段位差 2 段，每局让 4 分）。 让分叠加原则：若对阵双方同时存在性别差和段位差，让分数为两项之和（例如：初级 3 段男选手 vs 初级 1 段女选手，性别让 2 分 + 段位差 2 段让 4 分，共让 6 分，女选手开局 6:0 领先）。 （3）排名规则 全程采用单败淘汰赛，所有选手不分男女，通过抽签确定对阵顺序，胜者晋级下一轮，败者淘汰，决出前 8 名并进行排名。 中级组混合单打规则 （1）赛制与晋级规则 赛制：采用三局两胜制，每局 11 分制（让分后先得 11 分者胜，需领先对手 2 分及以上）。 赛制流程：全程采用单败淘汰赛，所有选手不分男女，通过抽签确定对阵顺序，胜者晋级下一轮，败者淘汰，直至决出前 8 名并进行排名。 种子设置：不设种子选手，抽签结果公平随机。 （2）让分规则 性别让分：当男选手对阵女选手时，男选手每局让 2 分。 段位让分：中级组内，高段位选手对阵低段位选手时，每高 1 段让 2 分（如中级 2 段 vs 中级 1 段，让 2 分；中级 3 段 vs 中级 1 段，让 4 分）。 让分叠加原则：若对阵双方同时存在性别差和段位差，让分数为两项之和。 （3）排名规则 全程采用单败淘汰赛，所有选手不分男女，通过抽签确定对阵顺序，胜者晋级下一轮，败者淘汰，决出前 8 名并进行排名。 补充说明 段位认定：以之前举行的通级达标赛段位考核结果为准，参赛前需完成资格审查。 其他未明确事项由裁判组现场裁决。 九、录取名次及奖励 （一）完成本次通级达标赛（段位赛）的个人，可认证通识体育课体育竞赛参赛分数； （二）通过相应段位考核的个人，可获得对应名次的奖状以及奖品。 十、报名与报到 （一）实行网上推送问卷报名。各参赛人员需要扫码填写报名问卷，于 2026 年 5 月 9 日 11：59 前完成报名。 （报名二维码） （QQ 群二维码） （二）参赛运动员应该按照指定时间提前十分钟，自带球拍和衣物到达指定考点接受考核，如考核开始后十分钟仍未到场，则视为弃权，并计入系列赛事诚信档案。 十一、其他事项 （一）各参赛人员自行解决用餐，大会不提供用餐服务。 （二）通级达标赛考核期间，请各参赛人员保持场地整洁，不随意挪动挡板，不丢弃垃圾，不携带食品入场，文明比赛，文明观看，有序入场。 （三）如有任何疑惑，请联系组委会联系人：阮子蒙，QQ 2848586753；邮箱：12412207@mail.sustech.edu.cn。 十二、本规程的解释权属南方科技大学乒乓球社。 十三、未尽事宜由承办单位另行通知。 南方科技大学乒乓球通级达标赛组委会 2026 年 4 月 20 日 2026 SUSTech 4th Table Tennis Skill Level Qualifying (Ranking) Competition Competition Regulations I. Organizer Sports Center, Southern University of Science and Technology (SUSTech) II. Undertaker SUSTech Table Tennis Club III. Competition Date May 16, 2026 IV. Competition Venues Comprehensive Training Gymnasium & Table Tennis Hall on the 2nd Floor of Student Activity Center V. Competition Event Table Tennis Skill Level (Elementary, Intermediate) Qualifying (Ranking) Competition VI. Eligible Participants SUSTech students who have obtained Elementary or Intermediate certificates in the Table Tennis Skill Level Qualifying Competition VII. Entry Rules Online registration via questionnaire QR code is required. All participants must complete registration by 11:59 on May 9, 2026. The organizing committee will select the first 64 registered students (32 for Elementary Level, 32 for Intermediate Level) based on questionnaire submission time and intended participation time. All participants must be in good health. Those injured or unfit may withdraw; other situations during the competition will be handled by the organizing committee. Eligible individuals who fail to register on time will be deemed to have forfeited, with no replacements. Registered participants who cannot attend on time must apply for cancellation before 11:59 on May 9, 2026 via email: 12412207@mail.sustech.edu.cn; failure to cancel will be recorded in the series competition integrity file. VIII. Competition Rules The competition follows the Implementation Measures for SUSTech Table Tennis Skill Level (Ranking) Assessment (Trial), SUSTech Table Tennis Skill Level (Elementary) Evaluation Criteria and SUSTech Table Tennis Skill Level (Intermediate) Evaluation Criteria. Participants are divided into Elementary Group and Intermediate Group according to their acquired skill levels; they must register for the corresponding group only. Specific rules are as follows: Group Division & Eligibility Grouping Principle: According to the Implementation Measures for SUSTech Table Tennis Skill Level (Ranking) Assessment (Trial), participants are grouped by their acquired levels: Elementary Group: Participants who have passed Elementary Level (including Elementary 1-Dan, 2-Dan, 3-Dan); Intermediate Group: Participants who have passed Intermediate Level (including Intermediate 1-Dan, 2-Dan, 3-Dan); Those without any level certificate must first pass the qualifying assessment before registering for the corresponding group. Registration Requirement: Each participant may enter only one group; cross-group participation is prohibited. Elementary Group Mixed Singles Rules (1) Match Format & Advancement Format: Best of 3 games, 11 points per game (first to 11 points with a 2-point lead after handicap). System: Single elimination; all participants draw lots for match-ups regardless of gender; winners advance, losers are eliminated until top 8 are determined and ranked. Seeding: No seeded players; random draw. (2) Handicap Rules Gender Handicap: Male players give 2 points per game to female players. Ranking Handicap: In the same group, higher-ranked players give 2 points per rank per game to lower-ranked players (e.g., Elementary 3-Dan vs Elementary 1-Dan: 4 points per game). Combined Handicap: Handicaps are added if both gender and ranking differences apply (e.g., Elementary 3-Dan male vs Elementary 1-Dan female: 2 + 4 = 6 points; female starts at 6:0). (3) Ranking Rules Single elimination with random draw; winners advance, losers are eliminated; top 8 are ranked. Intermediate Group Mixed Singles Rules (1) Match Format & Advancement Format: Best of 3 games, 11 points per game (first to 11 points with a 2-point lead after handicap). System: Single elimination; all participants draw lots for match-ups regardless of gender; winners advance, losers are eliminated until top 8 are determined and ranked. Seeding: No seeded players; random draw. (2) Handicap Rules Gender Handicap: Male players give 2 points per game to female players. Ranking Handicap: In Intermediate Group, higher-ranked players give 2 points per rank per game to lower-ranked players (e.g., Intermediate 2-Dan vs Intermediate 1-Dan: 2 points; Intermediate 3-Dan vs Intermediate 1-Dan: 4 points). Combined Handicap: Handicaps are added if both gender and ranking differences apply. (3) Ranking Rules Single elimination with random draw; winners advance, losers are eliminated; top 8 are ranked. Supplementary Notes Ranking Verification: Based on results of previous qualifying assessments; qualification check is required before competition. Any unmentioned matters will be decided by the referees on site. IX. Awards & Recognition Participants who complete the competition will receive general education PE competition credits. Those who pass the corresponding level assessment will be awarded certificates and prizes according to their rankings. X. Registration & Check-in Online registration via questionnaire QR code is required. All participants must complete registration by 11:59 on May 9, 2026. (Registration QR Code) (QQ Group QR Code) Participants must arrive at the designated venue 10 minutes early with their own racket and clothing for check-in. Those who are more than 10 minutes late after the start time will be deemed to have forfeited and recorded in the integrity file. XI. Other Provisions Participants arrange their own meals; no meal service is provided. Keep the venue clean during the competition: do not move barriers, litter, or bring food in; compete and spectate politely. For inquiries, contact the organizing committee: Ruan Zimeng, QQ 2848586753; Email: 12412207@mail.sustech.edu.cn. XII. Interpretation Right The interpretation of these regulations belongs to SUSTech Table Tennis Club. XIII. Supplementary Matters Any unfinished matters will be announced separately by the undertaker. Organizing Committee of SUSTech Table Tennis Skill Level Qualifying Competition April 20, 2026"

According to the university’s arrangement, the holiday will last from May 1 (Friday) to May 5 (Tuesday), totaling 5 days, classes will resume as normal on May 6 (Wednesday). To ensure students' personal and property safety, we hereby issue the following safety reminders: I. Abide by Laws and Regulations, Demonstrate Good Conduct Observe laws and regulations: Consciously abide by national laws and regulations as well as school rules. Actively practice the core socialist values, uphold social morality, and fully demonstrate the outstanding spirit of SUSTech students. Return to school on time: Students are requested to return to school on time within the specified period according to the university's teaching plan. If you are unable to return on time due to special circumstances, you must go through the leave procedures in a timely manner. II. Safe Travel and Risk Precaution Pay attention to weather changes and prevent risks scientifically: According to the weather forecast, a new round of rainfall is expected in southern China in the coming days. There will be a brief break on May 1st, but rain will return afterwards and persist through the Labor Day holiday. As the weather is changeable during the holiday, please stay updated on the latest weather forecasts and disaster warnings. When entering public places, familiarize yourself in advance with the evacuation routes and the locations of firefighting equipment. In case of fire, immediately dial 119 for emergency assistance, remain calm, and evacuate in an orderly manner. Strengthen safety awareness and travel in a civilized and green way: Enhance traffic safety awareness and advocate for the concepts of civilized courtesy and green travel. Do not take vehicles, ships, or other means of transportation without licenses or illegal operations. When riding an e-bike, be sure to wear a safety helmet correctly, drive in the non-motorized vehicle lane, do not run red lights, and do not go against the traffic. Report your itinerary and keep communication smooth: Students who leave the city where the university is located or travel to other places should report their itinerary to their counselors in advance. During the holiday, keep your mobile phone open and stay in touch with your family and counselors. III. Campus Safety and Risk Elimination Regulated campus access: Do not proxy report or lend access rights to others. Strictly prohibit involvement in paid proxy entry services. When receiving visitors, verify their identity and purpose to maintain campus order. Check before leaving: Before leaving your dorm or campus, lock doors and windows, turn off taps and power, unplug chargers, and avoid prolonged charging of phones/power banks to eliminate hazards. Observe dormitory rules: Follow electrical safety regulations. Open flames, illegal appliances, and private wiring are strictly prohibited. Do not bring e-bike batteries into dormitories, nor charge or store them indoors. Overnight stays of non-residents without approval are prohibited. IV. Stay Alert Against Fraud Beware of telecom scams: Stay vigilant when receiving calls or messages from strangers requesting bank details, codes, transfers, friend requests, link-clicking, or screen sharing. Pay special attention to scams posing as airline staff claiming ticket changes/refunds. Protect personal information: Do not lend ID documents or disclose personal/family information casually. Use official ticket channels: Purchase scenic, performance, train, or flight tickets only through official channels to avoid losses. V. Respond Scientifically and Seek Help Promptly Early detection and reporting: Report any suspicious or abnormal situations to school authorities immediately. Quick response in emergencies: In case of emergencies, contact your dorm supervisor, college counselor, or call the following hotlines: University General Duty Line: 0755-88012114 Campus Property Services: 0755-88010123 Community Health Center (on duty): 18218715551 24-hour Emergency: 0755-88010110 / 18566211101 Care for mental health: If you feel emotionally distressed or need urgent psychological support, you may seek help through: Phone counseling: 24-hour hotline 400-098-2525 or 400-098-0708 ext. 9 (professional counselors, free, confidential). QQ online counseling: Add QQ 2853301071 (verify with school name and student ID) for 24-hour service (free, confidential). Shenzhen Psychological Aid and Crisis Intervention Hotline (Kangning Hospital): 400-995-995-9 / 0755-25629459. We wish all of you a safe and fulfilling Labor Day holiday! Office of Student Affairs April 28, 2026"

Host SUSTech Sports Center 2. Co-organizers SUSTech Pickleball Team, Pickleball Club 3. Events Men‘s Singles, Women’s Singles, Men‘s Doubles 4. Participants All SUSTech undergraduates and postgraduates 5. Time & Venue Date: May 17, 2026, 15:00–18:00 Venue: Pickleball Court, Wind & Rain Gymnasium, SUSTech 6. Participation Requirements (1) Eligibility: Only students who have obtained a Level 2 or higher certificate in the SUSTech Pickleball Grading Standard Test (from 2024, 2025, or 2026) may participate. Each athlete may register for at most one event. (2) The Sports Center provides rackets and balls. Participants may also bring their own rackets. (3) The competition adopts a knockout format. Participants must arrive at the venue 30 minutes before the start for the draw. (4) Campus ID card is required for verification. Any cheating (e.g., impersonation) will lead to disqualification. 7. Competition Format (1) The competition is governed by the latest 2025 Pickleball Rules. (2) All matches use rally scoring. Matches are best of three games, each game to 15 points, win by 2 with a cap at 17 points. (3) If fewer than 8 players/teams enter an event, a round-robin format will be used. Each match is one game to 21 points, win by 2 with a cap at 23 points. In case of a tie in the round-robin standings, the winner will be determined by point differential. (4) If fewer than 3 players or 3 teams register for an event, that event will be cancelled. (5) Key rules: The serve must be made with an underhand motion, and the contact point between the paddle and ball must be below the waist. The “Two-Bounce Rule” applies: after the serve, the receiving side must let the ball bounce once on their side before returning it; then the serving side must also let the ball bounce once on their side before playing the return. After these two bounces, either side may volley (hit the ball before it bounces). When the server is on the right side, the score is even; when on the left side, the score is odd. (6) Event Schedule 1) Registration closes on May 16. Eligible players must scan the QR code in the WeChat group to complete the registration questionnaire. 2) The draw or group allocation will take place half an hour before the start of the competition on May 17. 8. Registration Eligible participants should scan the QR code to join the WeChat group. Registration will be done via an online questionnaire. Further competition information will be released through the group. Contact: Teacher Wei, 0755-88010581 9. Awards (1) The top three finishers in each event will receive certificates and prizes. (2) Completing the event qualifies for PE course competition credits. 10. Officials and Referees Officials and referees will be appointed by the Organizing Committee. Any matters not covered herein will be announced separately."

In this campus life where you can neither push harder nor lie flat peacefully, do you often feel: Exhausted even without doing much? Overwhelmed by sudden emotions—anxiety, low mood, and irritability coming and going? Procrastinating until the last minute and then blaming yourself fiercely? Understanding all the principles but just unable to feel happy? If any of these resonate with you, what you may need is not \"trying harder\", but a new inner capacity—mindfulness. This Course Is for You If You are troubled by emotions such as anxiety, low mood, and irritability You want to live in harmony with your emotions instead of being controlled by them You want a safe and quiet space for yourself amid busyness and pressure This is a mindfulness group course designed exclusively for college students with emotional distress. No judgment, no empty slogans, no forced positivity. Course Introduction： Developed by Researcher Liu Xinghua from Peking University, Mindfulness Intervention for Emotional Distress (MIED) integrates classic mindfulness group programs with modern psychological therapies. Research has proven this course effectively alleviates anxiety, depression, and other emotional disturbances (Ju et al., 2022; Li et al., 2023; He et al., 2023; Wang et al., 2024).Many students who took the course reported noticeable improvements in mood, sleep, stress levels, and physical well-being. What You Will Gain in 8 Weeks： Befriend your emotions and reduce internal friction Improve focus, reduce procrastination, and boost learning efficiency Become more self-accepting and understanding of others Make mindfulness a gentle daily habit Course Details Venue: On-campus classroom (specific location to be announced after registration) Time: May 14 – July 2, 2026, every Thursday 19:00–21:00 (8 weeks total) Format: Offline small group + daily short practice (≈15 minutes per day) Language: Taught in Chinese Course Schedule Week Date Theme 1 May 19 (Tue) Meeting Mindfulness, Engaging in Life 2 May 26 (Tue) The Functions and Values of Emotions 3 June 2 (Tue) Sources and Solutions to Emotional Distress 4 June 9 (Tue) Willingly Acknowledge Uncomfortable Feelings 5 June 16 (Tue) Regulating Emotional Behaviors 6 June 16 (Tue) Thoughts Are Just Thoughts 7 June 30 (Tue) Gradually Facing Avoided Situations 8 July 7 (Tue) An Ending Is Also a Beginning What You Will Take Away Outstanding Student Certificate for completing the MIED program (attendance ≥ 7 sessions) More stable emotions, less anxiety and inner conflict A more focused mind and a gentler heart Instructor Chai Shuqiong Certified MIED Course Instructor Registered Psychologist & Psychotherapist National Second-Class Psychological Counselor Registration Process 1.Fill in the application form (scan the QR code below) https://wj.qq.com/s2/26495498/bzth/ 2.Schedule a 20-minute interview: share your current state and expectations 3.Confirm group admission and start your 8-week mindfulness journey Final Reminder This is an educational group counseling program and not a substitute for psychological counseling or therapy.To maintain a supportive group atmosphere, please attend as many sessions as possible.Course effectiveness depends greatly on daily practice—please set aside 15–30 minutes each day for practice.
